====================================================================================================
WRONG GLOBAL SYMBOL RESOLUTION REPORT
====================================================================================================

Detects potential Watcom 1-based indexing bugs where the decompiler resolves
a memory access to the wrong overlapping global. Globals that appear in the
.cpp pseudocode but NOT in the function's JSON reference list are flagged as
suspicious, since the reference list uses exact address resolution.

----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
SUMMARY
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  Functions checked: 5725
  Functions with suspicious globals: 1102
  Total suspicious global references: 1968

  Most commonly misresolved globals:

    g_CDemonActorClassInfo                             41 functions
    g_CEnemyClassInfo                                  30 functions
